/* <![CDATA[ */

            jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
                // Apply coupon button click
                $(document).on("click", ".apply-coupon-button", function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    
                    $.ajax({
                        type: "POST",
                        url: "https://warrior-makers.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php",
                        data: {
                            action: "apply_weekly_coupon",
                            coupon_code: "WEEKLYSPECIAL"
                        },
                        success: function(response) {
                            if (response.success) {
                                alert(response.data.message);
                                // Refresh the cart if necessary
                                if ($("body").hasClass("woocommerce-cart")) {
                                    window.location.reload();
                                }
                            } else {
                                alert(response.data.message);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                });
            });
        
//# sourceURL=jquery-js-after
/* ]]> */
</script>

    <script>
        jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
            const sliderWrapper = $(".custom-top-banner .fusion-column-wrapper");
            
            // Use custom arrow elements instead of text
            sliderWrapper.append('<div class="slider-arrow left"></div>');
            sliderWrapper.append('<div class="slider-arrow right"></div>');
            
            let currentIndex = 0;
            const sliderContent = $(".custom-top-banner .fusion-column-wrapper .fusion-text");
            const totalItems = sliderContent.length;
            let autoplayInterval;
            let isHovered = false;
            
            // Setup slider structure
            sliderContent.addClass("slider-item");
            sliderWrapper.prepend('<div class="slider-content"></div>');
            sliderContent.detach().appendTo(".custom-top-banner .slider-content");
            
            function showSlide(index) {
                // Ensure index is within bounds
                if (index < 0) index = totalItems - 1;
                if (index >= totalItems) index = 0;
                currentIndex = index;
                $(".custom-top-banner .slider-content").css("transform", `translateX(-${index * 100}%)`);
            }
            
            function nextSlide() {
                showSlide(currentIndex + 1);
            }
            
            function prevSlide() {
                showSlide(currentIndex - 1);
            }
            
            function startAutoplay() {
                // Clear any existing interval first
                if (autoplayInterval) {
                    clearInterval(autoplayInterval);
                }
                if (!isHovered) {
                    autoplayInterval = setInterval(nextSlide, 6000);
                }
            }
            
            function stopAutoplay() {
                if (autoplayInterval) {
                    clearInterval(autoplayInterval);
                    autoplayInterval = null;
                }
            }
            
            // Event handlers for manual navigation
            $(".custom-top-banner .slider-arrow.right").click(function() {
                nextSlide();
                stopAutoplay();
                setTimeout(startAutoplay, 100); // Restart autoplay after manual navigation
            });
            
            $(".custom-top-banner .slider-arrow.left").click(function() {
                prevSlide();
                stopAutoplay();
                setTimeout(startAutoplay, 100); // Restart autoplay after manual navigation
            });
            
            // Improved hover handling
            sliderWrapper.mouseenter(function() {
                isHovered = true;
                stopAutoplay();
            }).mouseleave(function() {
                isHovered = false;
                startAutoplay();
            });
            
            // Initialize the slider
            showSlide(0);
            
            // Only show the banner after everything is set up
            setTimeout(function() {
                $(".custom-top-banner").css("display", "block");
                startAutoplay(); // Start autoplay after banner is visible
                // Fade in arrows after a slight delay
                setTimeout(function() {
                    $(".custom-top-banner .slider-arrow").addClass('visible');
                }, 200);
            }, 100);
        });
    </script><script>

<p>1.If your order does not arrive (and you gave us the correct and valid shipping info), we will send you a<br />
new order to replace it.<br />
2. We do not offer returns at all, IF there is a problem with your product please reach out and tell us. We<br />
will decide if it warrants us sending you a replacement product or not. (pictures always help)<br />
3. If we deem there is a valid issue w/ your product which is not your fault, we will send a replacement asap.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="fusion-panel panel-default panel-1217cf0894193f6bc fusion-toggle-has-divider" style="--awb-title-color:var(--awb-color8);--awb-content-color:var(--awb-color8);"><div class="panel-heading"><h4 class="panel-title toggle" id="toggle_1217cf0894193f6bc"><a aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="1217cf0894193f6bc" role="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-parent="#accordion-1247-1" data-target="#1217cf0894193f6bc" href="#1217cf0894193f6bc"><span class="fusion-toggle-icon-wrapper" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-fusion-box active-icon awb-icon-minus" aria-hidden="true"></i><i class="fa-fusion-box inactive-icon awb-icon-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></span><span class="fusion-toggle-heading">I do not have the payment instructions?</span></a></h4></div><div id="1217cf0894193f6bc" class="panel-collapse collapse " aria-labelledby="toggle_1217cf0894193f6bc"><div class="panel-body toggle-content fusion-clearfix">
<p>Immediately after you submitted your order an email was sent to your contact information with the<br />
payment instructions, it also included your order# and Order total$. Find that email and follow those instructions.</p>
<p>If you selected at checkout to pay via CC/DC, you would have been emailed a custom payment link to the email you provided.</p>
<p><strong data-olk-copy-source="MessageBody">ALL Payment Links are sent once daily, during the hours of 8AM-11AM PST.</strong><br />
If your order was placed anytime within the preceding 24 hours of that window, that is when you will receive your payment link. Orders placed after 8AM will receive their Payment Link the following morning during that same window.<br />
<em>Links do not expire, so you may pay at a time of your convenience.</em></p>

<p>If you paid for <strong>ROUTE Shipping Insurance</strong> to file a claim simply go to: <a href="https://claims.route.com/">https://claims.route.com/</a>. <strong><br /></strong></p>
<p><strong>RULES:</strong> If your package has been delayed even by 2 days, you can file a claim and it will not be denied, Route will resend you a brand new order even if your order still ends up arriving. Always file a claim if it is lost, delayed, or damaged in any way.</p>

website that has a peptide reconstitution calculator and more information.<br />
https://www.peptideresearchinstitute.org/</p>
</div></div></div><div class="fusion-panel panel-default panel-9e0b8559cb407f403 fusion-toggle-has-divider" style="--awb-title-color:var(--awb-color8);--awb-content-color:var(--awb-color8);"><div class="panel-heading"><h4 class="panel-title toggle" id="toggle_9e0b8559cb407f403"><a aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="9e0b8559cb407f403" role="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-parent="#accordion-1247-1" data-target="#9e0b8559cb407f403" href="#9e0b8559cb407f403"><span class="fusion-toggle-icon-wrapper" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-fusion-box active-icon awb-icon-minus" aria-hidden="true"></i><i class="fa-fusion-box inactive-icon awb-icon-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></span><span class="fusion-toggle-heading">Do I need Bacteriostatic water or syringes?</span></a></h4></div><div id="9e0b8559cb407f403" class="panel-collapse collapse " aria-labelledby="toggle_9e0b8559cb407f403"><div class="panel-body toggle-content fusion-clearfix">
<p>We legally cannot give medical advice nor any advice relating to use. Our peptides are sold as “research<br />
peptides” only. There are various available resources to answer your questions, this is an example of<br />
one. https://www.peptideresearchinstitute.org/</p>
<p>We do provide Bacteriostatic Water with every peptide for research purposes</p>
</div></div></div><div class="fusion-panel panel-default panel-ba68ffe12556d0e8e fusion-toggle-has-divider"><div class="panel-heading"><h4 class="panel-title toggle" id="toggle_ba68ffe12556d0e8e"><a aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ba68ffe12556d0e8e" role="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-parent="#accordion-1247-1" data-target="#ba68ffe12556d0e8e" href="#ba68ffe12556d0e8e"><span class="fusion-toggle-icon-wrapper" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-fusion-box active-icon awb-icon-minus" aria-hidden="true"></i><i class="fa-fusion-box inactive-icon awb-icon-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></span><span class="fusion-toggle-heading">Do I receive bacteriostatic water or syringes with my order?</span></a></h4></div><div id="ba68ffe12556d0e8e" class="panel-collapse collapse " aria-labelledby="toggle_ba68ffe12556d0e8e"><div class="panel-body toggle-content fusion-clearfix">
<p>Most all of our products that come as freeze dried powder do come w/ a 3ml Vial of bacteriostatic water.<br />
There are a few exceptions. Please look at the description of the product you purchased, it will tell you<br />
exactly what you will receive with your order. NO we will never provide syringes with any order.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="fusion-panel panel-default panel-b03dd63635ccf769c fusion-toggle-has-divider"><div class="panel-heading"><h4 class="panel-title toggle" id="toggle_b03dd63635ccf769c"><a aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="b03dd63635ccf769c" role="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-parent="#accordion-1247-1" data-target="#b03dd63635ccf769c" href="#b03dd63635ccf769c"><span class="fusion-toggle-icon-wrapper" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-fusion-box active-icon awb-icon-minus" aria-hidden="true"></i><i class="fa-fusion-box inactive-icon awb-icon-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></span><span class="fusion-toggle-heading">How much bacteriostatic water do I use to reconstitute?</span></a></h4></div><div id="b03dd63635ccf769c" class="panel-collapse collapse " aria-labelledby="toggle_b03dd63635ccf769c"><div class="panel-body toggle-content fusion-clearfix">
<p>The answer is different with every product, here is a link to an open source informational website that has<br />
a peptide reconstitution calculator and more information. https://www.peptideresearchinstitute.org/</p>
</div></div></div><div class="fusion-panel panel-default panel-2a450503c3e1cfff9 fusion-toggle-has-divider" style="--awb-title-color:var(--awb-color8);--awb-content-color:var(--awb-color8);"><div class="panel-heading"><h4 class="panel-title toggle" id="toggle_2a450503c3e1cfff9"><a aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="2a450503c3e1cfff9" role="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-parent="#accordion-1247-1" data-target="#2a450503c3e1cfff9" href="#2a450503c3e1cfff9"><span class="fusion-toggle-icon-wrapper" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-fusion-box active-icon awb-icon-minus" aria-hidden="true"></i><i class="fa-fusion-box inactive-icon awb-icon-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></span><span class="fusion-toggle-heading">What is your contact info?</span></a></h4></div><div id="2a450503c3e1cfff9" class="panel-collapse collapse " aria-labelledby="toggle_2a450503c3e1cfff9"><div class="panel-body toggle-content fusion-clearfix">
<p>Please submit any inquiries via the contact form located on our <a href="/contact">Contact page</a>.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="fusion-panel panel-default panel-7eccf00f1ec86ec45 fusion-toggle-has-divider" style="--awb-title-color:var(--awb-color8);--awb-content-color:var(--awb-color8);"><div class="panel-heading"><h4 class="panel-title toggle" id="toggle_7eccf00f1ec86ec45"><a aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="7eccf00f1ec86ec45" role="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-parent="#accordion-1247-1" data-target="#7eccf00f1ec86ec45" href="#7eccf00f1ec86ec45"><span class="fusion-toggle-icon-wrapper" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-fusion-box active-icon awb-icon-minus" aria-hidden="true"></i><i class="fa-fusion-box inactive-icon awb-icon-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></span><span class="fusion-toggle-heading">How long do peptides last for?</span></a></h4></div><div id="7eccf00f1ec86ec45" class="panel-collapse collapse " aria-labelledby="toggle_7eccf00f1ec86ec45"><div class="panel-body toggle-content fusion-clearfix">
<p>This answer varies on the peptide itself. Once peptides have been reconstituted generally the<br />
effectiveness of peptide reduces after 8 weeks even while refrigerated. There is more information available on the internet. Here’s an independent resource to read.<br />
https://www.peptideresearchinstitute.org/</p>

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const form = document.querySelector('.research-form');
  if (!form) return;

  const submitButton = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
  const instTypeSelect = document.getElementById('rg-inst-type');
  const otherInstRow = document.getElementById('other-inst-row');
  const otherInstInput = document.getElementById('rg-other-inst');
  
  const fields = form.querySelectorAll(
    'input[type="text"], input[type="email"], input[type="checkbox"], select'
  );

  // Show/hide "Other" field
  instTypeSelect.addEventListener('change', function() {
    if (this.value === 'Other') {
      otherInstRow.style.display = 'block';
      otherInstInput.required = true;
    } else {
      otherInstRow.style.display = 'none';
      otherInstInput.required = false;
      otherInstInput.value = '';
    }
    validateForm();
  });

  // Form validation
  function validateForm() {
    let valid = true;

    fields.forEach(field => {
      if (field.type === 'checkbox') {
        if (!field.checked) valid = false;
      } else if (field.tagName === 'SELECT') {
        if (!field.value) valid = false;
      } else {
        if (field.required && field.offsetParent !== null) {
          if (!field.value.trim()) valid = false;
        }
      }
    });

    if (!submitButton.classList.contains('submitting')) {
      submitButton.disabled = !valid;
      submitButton.classList.toggle('enabled', valid);
    }
  }

  fields.forEach(field => {
    field.addEventListener('input', validateForm);
    field.addEventListener('change', validateForm);
  });

  // Form submission
  form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (submitButton.classList.contains('submitting')) {
      return;
    }

    // Set loading state
    submitButton.textContent = 'Loading...';
    submitButton.disabled = true;
    submitButton.classList.add('submitting');
    submitButton.classList.remove('enabled');

    // Prepare data
    const submissionData = {
      firstName: document.getElementById('rg-fname').value,
      lastName: document.getElementById('rg-lname').value,
      email: document.getElementById('rg-email').value,
      institutionType: document.getElementById('rg-inst-type').value,
      institutionTypeOther: document.getElementById('rg-other-inst').value || ''
    };

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const MIN_LOADING_TIME = 1000; // Show loading for at least 1 second
    const MAX_WAIT_TIME = 3000; // After 3 seconds, let them through anyway
    
    let submissionComplete = false;

    // Function to close modal
    function closeModal() {
      const closeButton = document.querySelector(
        '.off-canvas-close.digital-close.close-position-right'
      );
      if (closeButton) {
        closeButton.click();
        // Save to localStorage AFTER modal closes successfully
        localStorage.setItem('research_disclaimer_accepted', 'true');
      }
    }

    // Timeout fallback - let them through after 3 seconds regardless
    const timeoutHandler = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!submissionComplete) {
        console.log('Submission timeout - letting user through anyway');
        closeModal();
      }
    }, MAX_WAIT_TIME);

    // Submit to server (continues in background even if timeout occurs)
    fetch('/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=save_disclaimer', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(submissionData)
    })
    .then(response => response.json())
    .then(data => {
      submissionComplete = true;
      clearTimeout(timeoutHandler);

      if (data.success) {
        console.log('✅ Submission successful! ID:', data.data.id);
        
        // Calculate remaining time to show loading (minimum 1 second)
        const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
        const remainingTime = Math.max(0, MIN_LOADING_TIME - elapsed);
        
        // Close modal after remaining time
        setTimeout(() => {
          closeModal();
        }, remainingTime);
        
      } else {
        throw new Error(data.data.message || 'Submission failed');
      }
    })
    .catch(error => {
      submissionComplete = true;
      clearTimeout(timeoutHandler);
      
      console.error('❌ Submission error:', error);
      
      // Check if we've been waiting long enough
      const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
      
      if (elapsed >= MIN_LOADING_TIME) {
        // Been at least 1 second - let them through anyway
        console.log('Letting user through despite error');
        setTimeout(() => {
          closeModal();
        }, 500);
      } else {
        // Less than 1 second - wait a bit then let them through
        setTimeout(() => {
          closeModal();
        }, MIN_LOADING_TIME - elapsed);
      }
    });
  });
});
</script></div></div></div></div>

        <script>
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                const wrappers = document.querySelectorAll('.tc-carousel-wrapper');
                
                if (wrappers.length === 0) return;
                
                wrappers.forEach(function(wrapper) {
                    const container = wrapper.querySelector('.tc-carousel-container');
                    const track = wrapper.querySelector('.tc-carousel-track');
                    if (!track) return;
                    
                    const items = track.querySelectorAll('.tc-carousel-item');
                    const counter = wrapper.querySelector('.tc-carousel-counter strong');
                    const totalElement = wrapper.querySelector('.tc-total-positions');
                    
                    if (items.length === 0) return;
                    
                    let currentPage = 0;
                    let isTransitioning = false;
                    
                    function getCardsPerView() {
                        // Use window.innerWidth instead of container width for true screen size detection
                        const screenWidth = window.innerWidth;
                        const mobileSlides = 1;
                        const desktopSlides = 3;
                        
                        if (screenWidth >= 900) {
                            return desktopSlides; // True desktop screens
                        } else {
                            return mobileSlides; // True mobile/tablet screens
                        }
                    }
                    
                    function getTotalPages() {
                        const cardsPerView = getCardsPerView();
                        return Math.max(1, items.length - cardsPerView + 1);
                    }
                    
                    function updateCarousel(animate = true) {
                        const cardsPerView = getCardsPerView();
                        const containerWidth = container.offsetWidth;
                        const gap = getGap();
                        const screenWidth = window.innerWidth; // Add this line
                        const mobileSlides = 1;
                        
                        // Use screenWidth instead of containerWidth for mobile detection
                        if (screenWidth < 900 && mobileSlides === 1) {
                            // Calculate card width for peek effect on mobile screens
                            let cardWidthPercent;
                            if (screenWidth <= 400) {
                                cardWidthPercent = 80; // 80% width on very small screens
                            } else if (screenWidth <= 768) {
                                cardWidthPercent = 82; // 82% width on small screens
                            } else {
                                cardWidthPercent = 85; // 85% width on medium screens
                            }
                            
                            const cardWidth = (containerWidth * cardWidthPercent / 100) - gap;
                            
                            // Set card widths
                            items.forEach(function(item) {
                                item.style.width = cardWidth + 'px';
                                item.style.minWidth = cardWidth + 'px';
                                item.style.maxWidth = cardWidth + 'px';
                            });
                            
                            // Calculate translation - move by full card width + gap for each page
                            const translateX = -(currentPage * (cardWidth + gap));
                            
                            // Set gap and transform
                            track.style.gap = gap + 'px';
                            
                            if (animate) {
                                track.style.transition = 'transform 0.3s ease';
                            } else {
                                track.style.transition = 'none';
                            }
                            
                            track.style.transform = 'translateX(' + translateX + 'px)';
                            track.offsetHeight; // Force reflow
                            
                        } else {
                            // Standard calculation for desktop or mobile with multiple slides
                            const totalGaps = (cardsPerView - 1) * gap;
                            const cardWidth = (containerWidth - totalGaps) / cardsPerView;
                            
                            // Calculate translation based on current page
                            const translateX = -(currentPage * (cardWidth + gap));
                            
                            // Set card widths consistently
                            items.forEach(function(item) {
                                item.style.width = cardWidth + 'px';
                                item.style.minWidth = cardWidth + 'px';
                                item.style.maxWidth = cardWidth + 'px';
                            });
                            
                            // Set gap
                            track.style.gap = gap + 'px';
                            
                            if (animate) {
                                track.style.transition = 'transform 0.3s ease';
                            } else {
                                track.style.transition = 'none';
                            }
                            
                            track.style.transform = 'translateX(' + translateX + 'px)';
                            track.offsetHeight; // Force reflow
                        }
                        
                        updateNavigation();
                    }

                    // Also update the getGap function to use screen width:
                    function getGap() {
                        const screenWidth = window.innerWidth; // Use window.innerWidth instead of container width
                        if (screenWidth <= 400) {
                            return 12;
                        } else if (screenWidth >= 900) {
                            return 24;
                        } else if (screenWidth <= 768) {
                            return 16;
                        } else {
                            return 20;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    function updateNavigation() {
                        const totalPages = getTotalPages();
                        
                        const lines = wrapper.querySelectorAll('.tc-nav-line');
                        lines.forEach(function(line, index) {
                            if (index === currentPage) {
                                line.classList.add('active');
                                line.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
                            } else {
                                line.classList.remove('active');
                                line.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false');
                            }
                        });
                        
                        if (counter) {
                            counter.textContent = String(currentPage + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                        }
                        if (totalElement) {
                            totalElement.textContent = String(totalPages).padStart(2, '0');
                        }
                    }
                    
                    function goToPage(pageIndex) {
                        const totalPages = getTotalPages();
                        const newPage = Math.max(0, Math.min(pageIndex, totalPages - 1));
                        
                        if (newPage !== currentPage && !isTransitioning) {
                            isTransitioning = true;
                            currentPage = newPage;
                            updateCarousel();
                            
                            setTimeout(function() {
                                isTransitioning = false;
                            }, 300);
                        }
                    }
                    
                    function goNext() {
                        const totalPages = getTotalPages();
                        if (currentPage < totalPages - 1) {
                            goToPage(currentPage + 1);
                        }
                    }
                    
                    function goPrev() {
                        if (currentPage > 0) {
                            goToPage(currentPage - 1);
                        }
                    }
                    
                    function initializeCarousel() {
                        const totalPages = getTotalPages();
                        const cardsPerView = getCardsPerView();
                        
                        // Only show navigation if we need it for current viewport
                        const navContainer = wrapper.querySelector('.tc-carousel-navigation');
                        const controlsContainer = wrapper.querySelector('.tc-carousel-controls');
                        
                        if (items.length <= cardsPerView) {
                            // Hide navigation if all items fit in view
                            if (controlsContainer) {
                                controlsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        } else {
                            // Show navigation and build it
                            if (controlsContainer) {
                                controlsContainer.style.display = 'flex';
                            }
                            
                            if (navContainer) {
                                navContainer.innerHTML = '';
                                
                                for (let i = 0; i < totalPages; i++) {
                                    const lineButton = document.createElement('button');
                                    lineButton.className = 'tc-nav-line-wrapper';
                                    
                                    const line = document.createElement('div');
                                    line.className = 'tc-nav-line' + (i === 0 ? ' active' : '');
                                    line.setAttribute('data-page', i);
                                    line.setAttribute('role', 'tab');
                                    line.setAttribute('aria-selected', i === 0 ? 'true' : 'false');
                                    line.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Go to page ' + (i + 1));
                                    
                                    lineButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
                                        goToPage(i);
                                    });
                                    
                                    lineButton.appendChild(line);
                                    navContainer.appendChild(lineButton);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        
                        updateCarousel(false);
                    }
                    
                    const images = track.querySelectorAll('img');
                    let imagesLoaded = 0;
                    const totalImages = images.length;
                    
                    function onImageLoad() {
                        imagesLoaded++;
                        if (imagesLoaded === totalImages) {
                            initializeCarousel();
                        }
                    }
                    
                    if (totalImages === 0) {
                        setTimeout(initializeCarousel, 100);
                    } else {
                        images.forEach(function(img) {
                            if (img.complete) {
                                onImageLoad();
                            } else {
                                img.addEventListener('load', onImageLoad);
                                img.addEventListener('error', onImageLoad);
                            }
                        });
                        
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            if (imagesLoaded < totalImages) {
                                initializeCarousel();
                            }
                        }, 2000);
                    }
                    
                    // Touch/swipe support
                    let startX = 0;
                    let isDragging = false;
                    
                    track.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
                        startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
                        isDragging = true;
                    }, { passive: true });
                    
                    track.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
                        if (!isDragging) return;
                        isDragging = false;
                        
                        const endX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
                        const diff = startX - endX;
                        
                        if (Math.abs(diff) > 50) {
                            if (diff > 0) {
                                goNext();
                            } else {
                                goPrev();
                            }
                        }
                    }, { passive: true });
                    
                    // Mouse drag support
                    let mouseStartX = 0;
                    let isMouseDragging = false;
                    
                    track.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
                        mouseStartX = e.clientX;
                        isMouseDragging = true;
                        track.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
                        e.preventDefault();
                    });
                    
                    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
                        if (!isMouseDragging) return;
                        e.preventDefault();
                    });
                    
                    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function(e) {
                        if (!isMouseDragging) return;
                        isMouseDragging = false;
                        track.style.cursor = 'grab';
                        
                        const endX = e.clientX;
                        const diff = mouseStartX - endX;
                        
                        if (Math.abs(diff) > 50) {
                            if (diff > 0) {
                                goNext();
                            } else {
                                goPrev();
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    
                    // Wheel/scroll support
                    let wheelTimeout;
                    container.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        
                        clearTimeout(wheelTimeout);
                        wheelTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                            if (e.deltaY > 0) {
                                goNext();
                            } else if (e.deltaY < 0) {
                                goPrev();
                            }
                        }, 50);
                    }, { passive: false });
                    
                    // Keyboard navigation
                    track.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            goPrev();
                        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            goNext();
                        }
                    });
                    
                    track.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
                    track.setAttribute('role', 'group');
                    track.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Testimonial carousel');
                    
                    // Window resize handler
                    let resizeTimeout;
                    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
                        clearTimeout(resizeTimeout);
                        resizeTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                            const totalPages = getTotalPages();
                            currentPage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages - 1);
                            
                            initializeCarousel(); // Reinitialize everything on resize
                        }, 100);
                    });
                });
            });
        </script>
